# NYC Restaurants Yelp Guide

## Atlantic Grill

**Description:** Just steps from Lincoln Center, this Upper West Side spot specializes in seafood of all sorts—oyster, clam and shellfish selections that change daily and market-fresh fish available simply grilled a la carte or as composed entrees.

Yelp categorizes this spot as New American, Seafood, and Sushi Bars. In Yelp reviews, service is mentioned in 47% of reviews, with one diner highlighting bartender Kayla's skill in making an excellent Cosmopolitan. Additionally, 4.5% of reviewers discuss the timely service, noting that the staff was attentive to ensure customers made it to a 7:30 curtain time.

**Neighborhood:** Upper West Side

**Address:** 50 W. 65th St., Manhattan, NY 10023

**Coordinates:** 40.7724966, -73.9811104

**Price:** $$$$

**Available:** delivery, pickup

**Michelin Award:** N/A

**NYT Top 100 Rank:** N/A

**Yelp Rating:** 3.7

**Yelp Review Count:** 683

**OpenTable ID:** 1175752

**Description:** Felice on Hudson fits seamlessly into the picturesque West Village, bringing an array of daily specials inspired by seasonal ingredients and an extensive Italian wine list to the neighborhood.

Yelp categorizes this spot as Bars and Italian. In Yelp reviews, service is mentioned in 42.9% of reviews, with diners noting the attentive nature of the staff. Food is discussed in 8.9% of reviews, highlighting the amazing arancini, al dente pasta, and great pizzeta.
